## MobileGPT

An Android client and Python-server agent that learns reusable app subtasks through exploration, selection, derivation and memory recall.

- Catalog URL: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-fclab-skku-mobilegpt/
- Original source: https://github.com/fclab-skku/MobileGPT
- Kind: agent
- Review: curated
- Scope: core
- Environments: android
- Topics: mobile-navigation, memory, planning, workflow
- Evidence checked: 2026-10-17T12:32:07Z
- ID: github:fclab-skku/mobilegpt

### Limitations

The old mobilegptsys repository resolves to fclab-skku/MobileGPT. Public Android client and Python server sources require Android SDK 33+, Accessibility Service setup and external service credentials. The README explicitly omits the random explorer and human-in-the-loop memory repair from the open-source version. Its benchmark description differs from the retained arXiv snapshot, so no dataset counts or reproduction claim are merged into this code record. External benchmark data was not approved as a separate release.

- Record JSON: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-fclab-skku-mobilegpt/record.json

## Mobster engine

iOS agent engine organized around an observe, decide, verify and act loop, using WebDriverAgent screen state and phone actions such as taps, typing, swipes and app switches.

- Catalog URL: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-radishsoftware-mobster/
- Original source: https://github.com/RadishSoftware/mobster
- Kind: agent
- Review: curated
- Scope: core
- Environments: ios
- Topics: mobile-navigation, evaluation
- Evidence checked: 2026-10-20T11:57:18.994340+00:00
- ID: github:radishsoftware/mobster

### Limitations

This entry covers the published engine, not the separate Mobster for Mac product. The documented reference example requires an already-serving WebDriverAgent runner for an iPhone and a model-service key; it performs a fixed read-only Settings task and declines approvals. The repository lists benchmark/evaluation tooling, but dated simulator subsets, real-device trials and earlier engine results are different evaluation populations. The README explicitly places several product modules outside this repository. Source inspection confirms WDA dispatch paths, not task success, security guarantees or reproducible performance; no phone, simulator, model or benchmark was run. MIT applies to the inspected repository software, not automatically to external WDA code, images or other data.

- Record JSON: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-radishsoftware-mobster/record.json

- weights: not recorded

This component record covers real_agents/web_agent and the documented WeBot Chrome-extension integration. The broader Data Agent and Plugins Agent are not treated as GUI-control assets. The source manifest includes the agent implementation and frontend/webot_extension.zip; the archive was not downloaded or executed. The README describes Docker deployment as under development and recommends source deployment. Apache-2.0 applies to project source; third-party extension/dependency notices remain separate.

## Yunkai

Android phone assistant combining model-driven tool use with accessibility-tree reading, screenshots, and tap, swipe and text-entry primitives. Its documented interaction flow presents operation plans for user confirmation.

- Catalog URL: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-solplum-yunkai/
- Original source: https://github.com/solplum/yunkai
- Kind: agent
- Review: curated
- Scope: core
- Environments: android
- Topics: mobile-navigation, screen-understanding, human-in-the-loop
- Evidence checked: 2026-10-20T11:57:12.431753+00:00
- ID: github:solplum/yunkai

### Limitations

The Android AccessibilityService contains concrete screen-reading and action paths. The inspected HarmonyOS accessibility extension is a lifecycle/event scaffold with its screen-content probe disabled; HDC appears in an external UI-test driver, so Harmony GUI-control parity is not established by this review. Setup requires a configured OpenAI-compatible model endpoint and API key: local storage does not establish offline inference or a no-transmission guarantee. Documented approval and privacy mechanisms are not independently tested security guarantees. No app, device test or model inference was run.

- Record JSON: https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-solplum-yunkai/record.json
